# Monthly Demand by Category

Endpoint: monthlyDemandByCategory

Endpoint Inputs (GraphQL Schema):

  "A list of data for creating the monthly demand by category chart."
  monthlyDemandByCategory(
    "The boundaries of each period to calculate demand for."
    periodBoundaries: [Instant!]!,
    "The identifiers of the sites to calculate the data for. Empty list will result in all sites."
    sites: [UUID!]!,
    "The part group category to stack by, if the stack type is PartGroupCategory."
    partGroupCategory: UUID,
    "The filters to filter by"
    filters: LineItemFilter!,
    "The stack type to calculate demand by."
    stackType: LineItemStackInput!,
    "The customer defined on time delivery buffer"
    onTimeDeliveryBuffer: Float!,
    "Whether to place the line items using the projected completion date or to use the due date"
    useProjectedCompletion: Boolean!
  ): [ProjectedDemandDataObject!]!

Endpoint Inputs (Typescript Document):

export type ChartsMonthlyDemandByCategoryArgs = {
  filters: LineItemFilter;
  onTimeDeliveryBuffer: Scalars['Float']['input'];
  partGroupCategory?: InputMaybe<Scalars['UUID']['input']>;
  periodBoundaries: Array<Scalars['Instant']['input']>;
  sites: Array<Scalars['UUID']['input']>;
  stackType: LineItemStackInput;
  useProjectedCompletion: Scalars['Boolean']['input'];
};

Endpoint Query & Query Variables (Typescript Document):

export type DemandByCategoryQueryVariables = Exact<{
  simulation: Scalars['UUID']['input'];
  stackType: LineItemStackInput;
  partGroupCategory?: InputMaybe<Scalars['UUID']['input']>;
  filters: LineItemFilter;
  periodBoundaries: Array<Scalars['Instant']['input']> | Scalars['Instant']['input'];
  sites: Array<Scalars['UUID']['input']> | Scalars['UUID']['input'];
  onTimeDeliveryBuffer: Scalars['Float']['input'];
  useProjectedCompletion: Scalars['Boolean']['input'];
}>;

export type DemandByCategoryQuery = { __typename?: 'Query', simulation: { __typename?: 'Simulation', charts: { __typename?: 'Charts', monthlyDemandByCategory: Array<{ __typename?: 'ProjectedDemandDataObject', startDate: DateTime, stackDataList: Array<{ __typename?: 'StackDataObject', name: string, quantity: number, value: number }> }> } } };

Return type (Typescript Document):

  /** A list of data for creating the monthly demand by category chart. */
  monthlyDemandByCategory: Array<ProjectedDemandDataObject>;

/** The data for the projected demand charts. */
export type ProjectedDemandDataObject = {
  __typename?: 'ProjectedDemandDataObject';
  /** The data for each stack in this period. */
  stackDataList: Array<StackDataObject>;
  /** The date this period starts. */
  startDate: Scalars['Instant']['output'];
};

/** The name for the stack, with the quantity and value of this stack. */
export type StackDataObject = {
  __typename?: 'StackDataObject';
  /** The name of the stack. */
  name: Scalars['String']['output'];
  /** The quantity of this stack. */
  quantity: Scalars['Float']['output'];
  /** The value of this stack. */
  value: Scalars['Float']['output'];
};
